## AAAHH!!! Real Monsters

      -> Infinite energy

      -> Infinite garbage

      -> Get 100 for each trash bag

      -> Infinite lives

  0BC7-17FC
      -> Start with 50 lives

      -> Start with 0 special scares
  FD69-37BF
      -> Start with 10 special scares
  4D69-37BF
      -> Start with 20 special scares

  D4CA-AF0D

      -> Monster books are worth 10
  DDCB-A7DD

      -> Most health power-ups worth more
